create or replace procedure MN_COLLECT_FEAT_SF_LOANSTYPE_OFFLINE
(in_table in VARCHAR2,
 out_table in VARCHAR2) 
AUTHID CURRENT_USER 
 

IS
sttm0 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm1 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm2 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm3 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm4 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm5 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm6 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm7 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm8 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm9 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm10 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm11 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm12 varchar2(32767 char);
sttm13 varchar2(32767 char);

-- Универсальная процедура для удаления таблицы, игнорируя ошибку, если таблица не существует
  PROCEDURE drop_table_if_exists(table_name IN VARCHAR2) IS
  BEGIN
    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'DROP TABLE ' || table_name;
  EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
      IF SQLCODE = -942 THEN
        NULL;  -- Игнорируем ошибку "таблица не существует"
      ELSE
        RAISE;  -- Для других ошибок выбрасываем исключение
      END IF;
  END drop_table_if_exists;
 
-- Процедура для удаления индекса если существует
  PROCEDURE drop_index_if_exists(index_name IN VARCHAR2) IS
  BEGIN
    EXECUTE IMMEDIATE 'DROP INDEX ' || index_name;
  EXCEPTION
    WHEN OTHERS THEN
      IF SQLCODE = -1418 THEN
        NULL; 
      ELSE
        RAISE;  
      END IF;
  END drop_index_if_exists;
  
 
BEGIN
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_appl_offline')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_maintype_offline')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_appl_offline_rtm')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_maintype_offline_rtm')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_maintype_offline_all')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_singleformattype_offline')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_raw')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_raw_rn')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_1')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_2')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_3')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_4')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(out_table, '_main')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(out_table, '_delq')));

drop_index_if_exists(upper('idx_'|| out_table || '_main'));
drop_index_if_exists(upper('idx_' || out_table || '_delq'));

execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_appl_offline as
select
    /*+ parallel(4) */
    a.client_did,
    a.report_date,
    b.applicationid,
    b.open_dt as appl_dt,
    row_number() over(partition by a.client_did, a.report_date order by b.open_dt desc) as rn
from '||in_table||' a
join dm.applications@db_space b
  on a.client_did = b.client_did
  and a.report_date > b.open_dt';
  
  
execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_appl_offline_rtm as
select /*+parallel(4)*/ distinct
        a.client_did,
        a.report_date,
        b.S1_ID applicationid,
        trunc(b.decision_date) as appl_dt,
        row_number() over(partition by a.client_did, a.report_date order by b.decision_date desc) as rn
from '||in_table||' a
join integro.int_rtmcvm_result@EXP_CVM b
        on a.client_did = b.client_did
        and a.report_date > trunc(b.decision_date)  --запросы строго до отчетной даты
'; 

execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_maintype_offline as
select /*+parallel(4)*/ distinct
        a.client_did, --id клиента
        a.report_date,
        a.appl_dt,
        a.applicationid, --id заявки
        m.applicationuid, --id заявки
        m.hjid,
        to_date(m.CREATEDATETIME, ''ddmmyyyyhh24miss'') as createdatetime
from '||in_table||'_sf_appl_offline a
join ods.ods_crp_sf_maintype m
        on to_char(a.applicationid) = ltrim(m.applicationuid,''0 '')
        and m.groupid is not null 
        and m.dml_type_cd <> ''D'' 
        and trunc(to_date(m.CREATEDATETIME, ''ddmmyyyyhh24miss'')) < a.report_date
where rn=1';

execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_maintype_offline_rtm as
select /*+parallel(4)*/ distinct
        a.client_did, --id клиента
        a.report_date,
        a.appl_dt,
        a.applicationid, --id заявки
        m.applicationuid, --id заявки
        m.hjid,
        to_date(m.CREATEDATETIME, ''ddmmyyyyhh24miss'') as createdatetime
from '||in_table||'_sf_appl_offline_rtm a
join ods.ods_crp_sf_maintype m
        on to_char(a.applicationid) = ltrim(m.applicationuid,''0 '')
        and m.groupid is not null 
        and m.dml_type_cd <> ''D'' 
        and trunc(to_date(m.CREATEDATETIME, ''ddmmyyyyhh24miss'')) < a.report_date
where rn=1';

execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_maintype_offline_all as
select /*+parallel(4)*/
a.*, row_number() over(partition by a.client_did, a.report_date order by a.appl_dt desc) as rn
from (
        select * from  '||in_table||'_sf_maintype_offline 
        union
        select /*+parallel(4)*/ * from '||in_table||'_sf_maintype_offline_rtm 
        ) a
';

execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_singleformattype_offline as 
select  /*+parallel(4)*/ distinct
        a.client_did,
        a.report_date,
        a.appl_dt,
        a.applicationid,
        a.applicationuid,
        a.createdatetime,
        sf.loans,
        sf.hjid,
        sf.load_dttm
from  '||in_table||'_sf_maintype_offline_all a
join ods.ods_crp_sf_singleformattype sf 
      on a.hjid = sf.main
      and sf.dml_type_cd <> ''D'' 
where a.rn=1
      ';
      
execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_raw as
select /*+parallel(4)*/ distinct 
       a.client_did,
       a.report_date,
       a.appl_dt, 
       a.applicationid,
       a.createdatetime,
       a.hjid, 
       a.loans,
       TTLDELQ30,                          --Количество просрочек до 30 дней +
       DELQBALANCE,                        --Просроченный баланс +
       INFOSOURCES,                        --Источники данных -
       to_date(OPENDATE, ''ddmmyyyyhh24miss'') as OPENDATE, --Дата открытия договора +
       STATUS,                             --Статус договора -
       ACCOUNT,                            --Номер договора -
       MAXDELQBALANCE,                     --Максимальный объем просроченной задолженности +
       OUTSTANDING,                         --Оставшаяся непогашенная задолженность +
       TTLDELQ3059,                         --Количество просрочек от 30 до 59 дня +
       PMTFREQ,                             --Код частоты платежей (1- 10) +
       to_date(INFCONFIRMDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as INFCONFIRMDATE, --Дата подтверждения информации по счету -
       CREDITLIMIT,                         --Кредитный лимит +
       NEXTPMT,                             --Размер следующего платежа +
       to_date(FINALPMTDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as FINALPMTDATE,  --Дата финального платежа (плановая) +
       TTLDELQ90PLUS,                       --Количество просрочек 90+ дней +
       RELATIONSHIP,                        --Тип отношения к договору (1-9) -
       TTLDELQ6089,                         --Количество просрочек от 60 до 89 дня +
       TYPE_,                               --Код типа договора (1-22,99) -
       MAXPAYMENT,                          --Максимальный ежемесячный платеж +
       TTLDELQ5,                            --Количество просрочек до 5 дней +
       ESTNEXTPMT,                          --Рассчитанный ежемесячный платёж +
       CURRENTDELQ,                         --Текущее количество дней просрочки +
       ESTCURRBALANCEAMT,                   --Рассчитанная общая выплаченная сумма, включая сумму основного долга, проценты, пени и штрафы +
       CURTOBASELIMIT,                      --Отношение кредитного лимита к первоначальному размеру лимита +
       CURRBALANCEAMT,                      --Общая выплаченная сумма, включая сумму основного долга, проценты, пени и штрафы +
       to_date(PMTSTRINGSTART,''ddmmyyyy'') as PMTSTRINGSTART,  --Дата начала строки PMT_STRING_84M +
       TTLDELQ529,                          --Общее число просрочек от 5 до 29 дней +
       to_date(FACTCLOSEDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as FACTCLOSEDATE, --Дата закрытия счета (фактическая) +
       ISOWN,                               --Признак своего кредита +
       CURRENCY,                            --Валюта договора (ISO буквенный код валюты) +
       to_date(LASTPAYMENTDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as LASTPAYMENTDATE,  --Дата последнего платежа +
       INTERESTRATE,                        --Процентная ставка по кредиту +
       COLLATERALCODE,                      --Тип залога -
       CREDITCOSTRATE,                      --Полная стоимость кредита (в процентах годовых) +
       BUSINESSCATEGORY,                    --Вид деятельности источника -
       TTL_DELQ_7,                          --Описание отсутствует
       TTL_DELQ_8_29 ,                      --Описание отсутствует
       PRINCIPAL_OUTSTANDING,              --Описание отсутствует
       PRINCIPAL_PAST_DUE,                  --Описание отсутствует
       INT_OUTSTANDING,                    --Описание отсутствует
       INT_PAST_DUE,                        --Описание отсутствует
       OTHER_OUTSTANDING,                   --Описание отсутствует
       OTHER_PAST_DUE,                    --Описание отсутствует
       TOTAL_AMOUNT_OF_FINANCE,             --Описание отсутствует
       CATEGORY_CODE,                       --Описание отсутствует
       PURPOSE_CODE,                        --Описание отсутствует
       IS_CONSUMER_LOAN,                    --Описание отсутствует
       IS_CARD_USED,                        --Описание отсутствует
       ENSURED_AMOUNT,                     --Описание отсутствует
       COBORROWERS_COUNT,                   --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_PRINCIPAL_DATE,            --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_INTEREST,                   --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_INTEREST_DATE,              --Описание отсутствует
       CREDIT_CARD_MIN_PMT,                 --Описание отсутствует
       INTEREST_PMT_FINAL_DATE,             --Описание отсутствует
       CREDIT_COST_DATE,                    --Описание отсутствует
       FUND_TRANSFER_DATE,                  --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_PRINCIPAL,                  --Описание отсутствует 
       uuid
from '||in_table||'_sf_singleformattype_offline a       
join ods.ods_crp_sf_loanstype lt 
     on lt.LoansTypes_Loan_Hjid = a.loans
     and lt.dml_type_cd <> ''D''
     and a.load_dttm < date ''2024-10-01''
     
union all         

select /*+parallel(4)*/ distinct 
       a.client_did,
       a.report_date,
       a.appl_dt, 
       a.applicationid,
       a.createdatetime,
       a.hjid, 
       a.loans,
       TTLDELQ30,                          --Количество просрочек до 30 дней +
       DELQBALANCE,                        --Просроченный баланс +
       INFOSOURCES,                        --Источники данных -
       to_date(OPENDATE, ''ddmmyyyyhh24miss'') as OPENDATE, --Дата открытия договора +
       STATUS,                             --Статус договора -
       ACCOUNT,                            --Номер договора -
       MAXDELQBALANCE,                     --Максимальный объем просроченной задолженности +
       OUTSTANDING,                         --Оставшаяся непогашенная задолженность +
       TTLDELQ3059,                         --Количество просрочек от 30 до 59 дня +
       PMTFREQ,                             --Код частоты платежей (1- 10) +
       to_date(INFCONFIRMDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as INFCONFIRMDATE, --Дата подтверждения информации по счету -
       CREDITLIMIT,                         --Кредитный лимит +
       NEXTPMT,                             --Размер следующего платежа +
       to_date(FINALPMTDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as FINALPMTDATE,  --Дата финального платежа (плановая) +
       TTLDELQ90PLUS,                       --Количество просрочек 90+ дней +
       RELATIONSHIP,                        --Тип отношения к договору (1-9) -
       TTLDELQ6089,                         --Количество просрочек от 60 до 89 дня +
       TYPE_,                               --Код типа договора (1-22,99) -
       MAXPAYMENT,                          --Максимальный ежемесячный платеж +
       TTLDELQ5,                            --Количество просрочек до 5 дней +
       ESTNEXTPMT,                          --Рассчитанный ежемесячный платёж +
       CURRENTDELQ,                         --Текущее количество дней просрочки +
       ESTCURRBALANCEAMT,                   --Рассчитанная общая выплаченная сумма, включая сумму основного долга, проценты, пени и штрафы +
       CURTOBASELIMIT,                      --Отношение кредитного лимита к первоначальному размеру лимита +
       CURRBALANCEAMT,                      --Общая выплаченная сумма, включая сумму основного долга, проценты, пени и штрафы +
       to_date(PMTSTRINGSTART,''ddmmyyyy'') as PMTSTRINGSTART,  --Дата начала строки PMT_STRING_84M +
       TTLDELQ529,                          --Общее число просрочек от 5 до 29 дней +
       to_date(FACTCLOSEDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as FACTCLOSEDATE, --Дата закрытия счета (фактическая) +
       ISOWN,                               --Признак своего кредита +
       CURRENCY,                            --Валюта договора (ISO буквенный код валюты) +
       to_date(LASTPAYMENTDATE,''ddmmyyyy'') as LASTPAYMENTDATE,  --Дата последнего платежа +
       INTERESTRATE,                        --Процентная ставка по кредиту +
       COLLATERALCODE,                      --Тип залога -
       CREDITCOSTRATE,                      --Полная стоимость кредита (в процентах годовых) +
       BUSINESSCATEGORY,                    --Вид деятельности источника -
       TTL_DELQ_7,                          --Описание отсутствует
       TTL_DELQ_8_29 ,                      --Описание отсутствует
       PRINCIPAL_OUTSTANDING,              --Описание отсутствует
       PRINCIPAL_PAST_DUE,                  --Описание отсутствует
       INT_OUTSTANDING,                    --Описание отсутствует
       INT_PAST_DUE,                        --Описание отсутствует
       OTHER_OUTSTANDING,                   --Описание отсутствует
       OTHER_PAST_DUE,                    --Описание отсутствует
       TOTAL_AMOUNT_OF_FINANCE,             --Описание отсутствует
       CATEGORY_CODE,                       --Описание отсутствует
       PURPOSE_CODE,                        --Описание отсутствует
       IS_CONSUMER_LOAN,                    --Описание отсутствует
       IS_CARD_USED,                        --Описание отсутствует
       ENSURED_AMOUNT,                     --Описание отсутствует
       COBORROWERS_COUNT,                   --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_PRINCIPAL_DATE,            --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_INTEREST,                   --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_INTEREST_DATE,              --Описание отсутствует
       CREDIT_CARD_MIN_PMT,                 --Описание отсутствует
       INTEREST_PMT_FINAL_DATE,             --Описание отсутствует
       CREDIT_COST_DATE,                    --Описание отсутствует
       FUND_TRANSFER_DATE,                  --Описание отсутствует
       NEXT_PMT_PRINCIPAL,                  --Описание отсутствует
       uuid
from '||in_table||'_sf_singleformattype_offline a       
join ods.ods_crp_sf_loanstype lt 
     on lt.sf_id  = a.hjid 
     and lt.dml_type_cd <> ''D''
     and a.load_dttm >= date ''2024-10-01'' ';
     
execute immediate '
create table '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_raw_rn as
select /*+parallel(4)*/ 
a.*,
case when FACTCLOSEDATE is not null and FACTCLOSEDATE < FINALPMTDATE then 1 else 0 end as dosroch_flg,
case when FACTCLOSEDATE is not null and FACTCLOSEDATE < FINALPMTDATE and (FACTCLOSEDATE - OPENDATE) <= 90 then 1 else 0 end as dosroch_3m_flg,
case when FACTCLOSEDATE is not null and FACTCLOSEDATE < FINALPMTDATE and (FACTCLOSEDATE - OPENDATE) <= 180 then 1 else 0 end  as dosroch_6m_flg,
row_number() over(partition by applicationid, uuid order by createdatetime desc) rn
from '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_raw a
where uuid is not null ';
      
sttm0 := 'create table '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_1 as
select /*+parallel(4)*/ 
client_did,
report_date,

--кол-во уникальных договоров
count(distinct case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_uuid_active_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 1 and FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_uuid_active_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 6 and FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_uuid_active_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 7 and FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_uuid_active_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 9 and FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_uuid_active_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 21 and FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_uuid_active_aggcnt, 

count(distinct uuid) as sf_loanstype_offline_uuid_total_aggcnt,
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 1 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_uuid_total_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 6 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_uuid_total_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 7 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_uuid_total_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 9 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_uuid_total_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 21 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_uuid_total_aggcnt, 
  
count(distinct case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_uuid_3yopen_aggcnt,
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_uuid_3yopen_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 6 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_uuid_3yopen_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 7 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_uuid_3yopen_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 9 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_uuid_3yopen_aggcnt, 
count(distinct case when TYPE_ = 21 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_uuid_3yopen_aggcnt, 

--количество досрочных погашений
count(distinct case when dosroch_flg = 1 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_aggcnt,   
count(distinct case when dosroch_flg = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_3yopen_aggcnt,

--доля досрочных погашений
count(distinct case when dosroch_flg = 1 then uuid else null end)/nullif(count(distinct uuid), 0) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_share,  
count(distinct case when dosroch_flg = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end)/nullif(count(distinct case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end),0) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_3yopen_share,

--количество досрочных погашений в течение 3 мес
count(distinct case when dosroch_3m_flg = 1 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_3m_aggcnt,   
count(distinct case when dosroch_3m_flg = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_3m_3yopen_aggcnt,

count(distinct case when dosroch_3m_flg = 1 then uuid else null end)/nullif(count(distinct uuid),0) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_3m_share, 
count(distinct case when dosroch_3m_flg = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end)/nullif(count(distinct case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end),0) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_3m_3yopen_share,

--количество досрочных погашений в течение 6 мес
count(distinct case when dosroch_6m_flg = 1 then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_6m_aggcnt,  
count(distinct case when dosroch_6m_flg = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_6m_3yopen_aggcnt,

count(distinct case when dosroch_6m_flg = 1 then uuid else null end)/nullif(count(distinct uuid),0) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_6m_share,  
count(distinct case when dosroch_6m_flg = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end)/nullif(count(distinct case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then uuid else null end),0) as sf_loanstype_offline_dosroch_6m_3yopen_share,

--Кредитный лимит 
SUM(case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then CREDITLIMIT else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_creditlimit_active_aggsum, 
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 1 and FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date  then CREDITLIMIT  else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_creditlimit_active_aggsum,

--Дата последнего платежа 
AVG(report_date-LASTPAYMENTDATE) sf_loanstype_offline_days_from_lastpmt_aggavg,
MAX(report_date-LASTPAYMENTDATE) sf_loanstype_offline_days_from_lastpmt_aggmax,
MIN(report_date-LASTPAYMENTDATE) sf_loanstype_offline_days_from_lastpmt_aggmin,

--Дата финального платежа (плановая)

--Дата закрытия счета (фактическая)

MAX(report_date-FACTCLOSEDATE) sf_loanstype_offline_days_from_factclose_aggmax,
MIN(report_date-FACTCLOSEDATE) sf_loanstype_offline_days_from_factclose_aggmin,

AVG(case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then (NEXT_PMT_PRINCIPAL_DATE - report_date) else null end) sf_loanstype_offline_days_to_nxtpmtprincipl_aggavg,

MAX(case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then (NEXT_PMT_INTEREST_DATE - report_date) else null end) sf_loanstype_offline_days_to_nxtpmtint_aggmax,
MIN(case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then (NEXT_PMT_INTEREST_DATE - report_date) else null end) sf_loanstype_offline_days_to_nxtpmtint_aggmin,

AVG(case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then (INTEREST_PMT_FINAL_DATE - report_date) else null end) sf_loanstype_offline_days_to_intpmtfinal_aggavg,

MIN(case when FACTCLOSEDATE is null and FINALPMTDATE > report_date then (INTEREST_PMT_FINAL_DATE - report_date) else null end) sf_loanstype_offline_days_to_intpmtfinal_aggmin

from '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_raw_rn
where rn =1 and currency = ''RUB''

SUM(case when TYPE_ = 9 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ6089 else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq6089_3yopen_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 21 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ6089 else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq6089_3yopen_aggsum,  

--Количество просрочек 90+ дней
AVG(TTLDELQ90PLUS) as sf_loanstype_offline_ttldelq90plus_total_aggavg,
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 1 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_ttldelq90plus_total_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 6 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_ttldelq90plus_total_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 7 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_ttldelq90plus_total_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 9 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq90plus_total_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 21 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq90plus_total_aggavg,  
  
MAX(TTLDELQ90PLUS) as sf_loanstype_offline_ttldelq90plus_total_aggmax,
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 1 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_ttldelq90plus_total_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 6 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_ttldelq90plus_total_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 7 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_ttldelq90plus_total_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 9 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq90plus_total_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 21 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq90plus_total_aggmax,  
 
SUM(TTLDELQ90PLUS) as sf_loanstype_offline_ttldelq90plus_total_aggsum, 
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 1 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_ttldelq90plus_total_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 6 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_ttldelq90plus_total_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 7 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_ttldelq90plus_total_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 9 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq90plus_total_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 21 then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq90plus_total_aggsum,  

AVG(case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggavg,
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 6 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 7 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 9 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggavg,  
AVG(case when TYPE_ = 21 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggavg,  

MAX(case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggmax,
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 6 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 7 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 9 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggmax,  
MAX(case when TYPE_ = 21 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggmax,  

SUM(case when opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggsum,
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 1 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_auto_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 6 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mort_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 7 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_cc_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 9 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_pil_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggsum,  
SUM(case when TYPE_ = 21 and opendate >= add_months(report_date, -36) then TTLDELQ90PLUS else null end) as sf_loanstype_offline_mf_ttldelq90plus_3yopen_aggsum

from  '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_raw_rn
where rn =1 and currency = ''RUB'' 
group by client_did, report_date';

from '||in_table||' a
left join '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_3 b
  on a.client_did = b.client_did
  and a.report_date = b.report_date
left join '||in_table||'_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_4 c
  on a.client_did = c.client_did
  and a.report_date = c.report_date
';

execute immediate sttm12||sttm13;

execute immediate 'grant select on '||out_table||'_delq to public';  
execute immediate
'
create index idx_'||out_table||'_delq on '||out_table||'_delq(client_did, report_date) parallel 4
';

drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_appl_offline')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_maintype_offline')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_appl_offline_rtm')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_maintype_offline_rtm')));

drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_singleformattype_offline')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_raw')));

drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_1')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_2')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_3')));
drop_table_if_exists(upper(concat(in_table, '_sf_loanstype_offline_feat_4')));

end;
